# Skills · J · part 9

845 entries, jim-theory-development to jira-integration. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **jim-theory-development**: Use when building or repairing the conceptual framework of a Journal of International Marketing (JIM) manuscript — theorizing why effects differ across countries, choosing cultural/institutional lenses, and drafting cross-national hypotheses. It builds the mechanism; jim-literature-positioning stakes the claim against prior work. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Marketing-Skills/skills/jim-theory-development) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jim-topic-selection**: Use when choosing or auditing a research question for a Journal of International Marketing (JIM) manuscript — testing whether the international dimension is the theoretical core and whether JIM beats sibling outlets. It vets the question; jim-theory-development builds the mechanism. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Marketing-Skills/skills/jim-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jim-workflow**: Use when starting or unblocking a Journal of International Marketing (JIM) manuscript and you need to pick the right jim-* sub-skill for the current stage, from first idea through the response letter. It routes to the specialist skills; it does not do their work. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Marketing-Skills/skills/jim-workflow) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jim-writing-style**: Use when drafting or revising the prose of a Journal of International Marketing (JIM) manuscript — the 200-word abstract, introduction architecture, precise culture language, and AMA-journal conventions. It polishes expression; substance fixes belong to the upstream skills. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Marketing-Skills/skills/jim-writing-style) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)

- **jimeng-douyin-cinematic-workflow**: 即梦 9:16 短视频工作流最终实战版。默认策略：当用户只说“生成一个视频”而未指定题目时，自动去抖音热榜抓一个题目，且优先选择最适合电影感叙事、画面感强、情绪转化空间大的题目，而不是机械地抓最热词。若用户明确指定题目，则优先使用用户题目。若用户明确说“直接执行”，则跳过 Stage A 展示确认，整条链路自动执行到底，并且必须在关键节点主动播报进度；一旦检测到后台任务状态发生变化，必须立即查询结果并主动回报给用户。固定流程：Stage A 四份文案确认 → 首帧 text2image → 尾帧 image2image → frames2video 生成 15 秒原片 → 优先回即梦做 AI 音效 → 分析视频主体人物选择旁白音色 → 写适配总时长的旁白文案 → 先生成旁白再按真实语音切字幕时间轴 → 添加电影级白色双语硬字幕 → 混音导出抖音发布版成片 → 自动生成发布配置并调用发布脚本全自动发抖音。 ([a23200/openclaw-config-backup](https://github.com/a23200/openclaw-config-backup/tree/HEAD/workspace/skills/jimeng-douyin-cinematic))

- **jimf-empirical-design**: Use when the cross-country/time-series data construction, sample, frequency, or measurement of a Journal of International Money and Finance (JIMF) manuscript is the bottleneck. Builds the international dataset and measurement; it does not establish causality (jimf-identification) or run robustness (jimf-robustness). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Money-and-Finance-Skills/skills/jimf-empirical-design) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jimf-identification**: Use when the identification argument is the bottleneck for a Journal of International Money and Finance (JIMF) manuscript — open-economy causal designs, high-frequency policy/FX surprises, capital-control natural experiments, or parameter identification in an open-economy model. Stress-tests the strategy to JIMF's international-finance bar. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Money-and-Finance-Skills/skills/jimf-identification) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jimf-internet-appendix**: Use when organizing the online/supplementary appendix and the data-and-code deposit for a Journal of International Money and Finance (JIMF) manuscript. Structures supplementary material and the replication package; it does not establish the main result or write the main text. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Money-and-Finance-Skills/skills/jimf-internet-appendix) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jimf-literature-positioning**: Use when staking a Journal of International Money and Finance (JIMF) manuscript's contribution against the international-finance frontier and its sibling journals. Sharpens positioning; it does not invent evidence, exemplars, or citations. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Money-and-Finance-Skills/skills/jimf-literature-positioning) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jimf-rebuttal**: Use when a Journal of International Money and Finance (JIMF) decision letter has arrived and you need a response-letter strategy for an R&R or a reject-and-resubmit. Plans the revision and response; it does not pre-empt objections before submission (jimf-referee-strategy). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Money-and-Finance-Skills/skills/jimf-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jimf-referee-strategy**: Use when anticipating and pre-empting referee objections before submitting a Journal of International Money and Finance (JIMF) manuscript. Maps the likely international-finance pushbacks to fixes you make now; it does not draft the post-decision response (jimf-rebuttal). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Money-and-Finance-Skills/skills/jimf-referee-strategy) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jimf-robustness**: Use when a Journal of International Money and Finance (JIMF) result must be shown stable across samples, regimes, measures, and inference choices. Designs the robustness layer mapped to international-finance threats; it does not establish the design (jimf-identification / jimf-empirical-design). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Journal-of-International-Money-and-Finance-Skills/skills/jimf-robustness) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **jimf-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for a Journal of International Money and Finance (JIMF) manuscript via Elsevier Editorial Manager — file set, abstract/Highlights/JEL, declarations (competing interest, CRediT), data statement, and house style.
